API 约定\n\n- 前缀 `/api`，JSON。前端一律用同源相对路径。\n- 认证：httpOnly cookie `session`；`POST /api/auth/*`；中间件 `requireAuth`、`requirePermission`。\n  cookie 属性为 `httpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/`，**不带 `Secure`**：交付以 HTTP 提供，带 `Secure` 会被浏览器丢弃。\n  会话失效语义（2026-09-29 修订，见 §9.11）：登出使当前会话立即失效；改密保留调用者当前会话、仅吊销该用户\n  其它会话（`revokeSessionsForUser(db, userId, { exceptSessionId })`）；找回密码吊销该用户全部会话且不自动登录\n  （`revokeSessionsForUser(db, userId)`）。三者的后续请求都立即按新状态判定。\n- 找回密码不泄露账户存在性：`forgot-password` 不查询账户，对已注册与未注册邮箱响应逐字节一致；\n  `reset-password` 对未知邮箱与错误验证码表现一致（`Verification code is invalid`，字段 `code`），任何路径都不修改账户。\n  字段错误仍按下面的 `errors[]` 规则一次返回（含 `code` 与密码字段错误）。\n  **有意保留的残留差异（2026-09-29，见 §9.12）**：验证码**正确**时，已注册邮箱得到密码字段错误或成功，未注册邮箱\n  在同样的密码字段错误前另有一条 `code` 错误（如 `[密码错误...]` vs `[code, 密码错误...]`）。这是需求内生的：验证码是页面\n  公开常量、恢复无服务端待处理状态、且恢复必须对已注册邮箱生效。**不得**为消除它而恢复“验证码错误时提前返回”——\n  那会丢掉 `errors[]` 一次返回全部字段错误，且不减少任何泄露。`reset-password` 成功响应只返回 `{ok, message}`，\n  **不得携带会话或账户数据**，否则该差异会从 1 bit 扩大。\n- 错误响应统一：`{ \"error\": { \"code\": string, \"message\": string, \"field\"?: string } }`，\n  字段级错误 `field` 用表单字段名；**只要存在字段错误就同时返回 `errors: [{code,message,field},...]`（单条也返回）**，\n  `error` 为其中的首项，前端统一按 `errors[]` 逐字段渲染。\n- 会话用户序列化为 `{id, username, email, emailVerified, createdAt}`，任何响应都不得包含 `password_hash`。\n- 资源命名：`/api/orgs`、`/api/repos/:owner/:name`、`/api/repos/:id/issues` 等；列表支持 `?q=&state=&label=`。\n\n## 4. 权限模型（backend/src/permissions，单一实现）\n\n```\ntype RepoRole = 'read'|'triage'|'write'|'maintain'|'admin'\neffectiveRepoRole(user, repo): RepoRole|null\n  - 个人仓库：owner = admin\n  - 组织仓库：org Owner → admin；否则 max(直接授权, 各团队授权)；团队层级不传播\n```\n\n写权限映射（共享决定，全部模块一致）：\n- 建分支/Web 编辑文件：write+\n- Issue 创建：write+；评论/编辑标题描述：write+\n- 指派/标签/里程碑/关闭重开 Issue：triage+（注意与创建不同）\n- PR 创建/比较：write+；评审（非作者）：write+；reviewers 请求：作者或 maintain+\n- 合并：maintain+；分支保护/Checks 状态/默认分支/可见性/删除：admin+\n- 组织：Owner = org admin；Member 仅可见性；团队维护仅 org Owner\n\n## 5. 内容模型（无真实 git 的简化模型，基础 PR 建表、M4 消费）\n\n```\nusers(id, username UNIQUE, email UNIQUE, password_hash, email_verified, created_at)\n                                                     -- email_verified 见 §9.10\nsessions(id, user_id, created_at)                    -- cookie 存 session id\norganizations(id, name UNIQUE, display_name, created_at)\norg_members(org_id, user_id, role['member'|'owner'])\nteams(id, org_id, name, description, parent_team_id NULL)\nteam_members(team_id, user_id)\nrepos(id, owner_user_id NULL, owner_org_id NULL, name, description,\n      visibility['public'|'private'], default_branch_id, fork_source_id NULL, created_at)\nrepo_grants(repo_id, subject_user_id NULL, subject_team_id NULL, role)  -- 每 subject 唯一\nbranches(id, repo_id, name UNIQUE(repo_id), head_commit_id)\ncommits(id, repo_id, parent_id NULL, author_id, message, created_at,\n        tree_json)   -- tree_json: {path: content} 全量快照（规模小，见 product-plan §5）\n                     -- parent_id = 第一父；合并 commit 的第二父见 commit_parents（§9.10）\ncommit_parents(commit_id, parent_commit_id, position)  -- 支持 §9.2 的双父合并 commit\ncommit_files(commit_id, path, status['added'|'modified'|'removed'], additions, deletions)\nissues(id, repo_id, number, title, body, author_id, state['open'|'closed'], created_at, updated_at)\ncomments(id, issue_id|pr_id, author_id, body, created_at)\nlabels(id, repo_id, name), issue_labels(issue_id, label_id)\nmilestones(id, repo_id, title), issue_milestones(issue_id PRIMARY KEY, milestone_id)\n                                                     -- 一个 issue 至多一个里程碑（§9.10）\nassignees(issue_id, user_id)\npull_requests(id, repo_id, number, title, body, author_id,\n      state['draft'|'open'|'closed'|'merged'], base_branch_id, compare_branch_id,\n      base_commit_id, compare_commit_id, merged_commit_id NULL, created_at)\nreviews(id, pr_id, reviewer_id, decision['comment'|'approve'|'request_changes'],\n        summary, compare_commit_id, created_at)   -- 同 reviewer 同 compare commit 取最新为有效\nreview_comments(id, pr_id, path, compare_commit_id, line, body, author_id,\n        published bool)                            -- pending = 未提交评审的草稿\ncheck_runs(id, pr_id, compare_commit_id, name['test'], status['pending'|'success'|'failure'],\n        setter_id, updated_at)\nbranch_protections(id, repo_id, branch_name UNIQUE(repo_id),\n        require_approval bool, require_check_test bool)\nactivities(id, kind, actor_id, subject_type, subject_id, payload_json, created_at) -- 时间线统一来源\n```\n\n- 写路径均在事务内；`activities` 同时服务 Issue/PR 时间线与审计。\n- branch name 合法性、`invalid..branch`、文件路径规则（不以 `/` 开头、无 `..` 段、不冲突）在后端统一校验。\n\n## 6. Seed 机制（契约）\n\n- `backend/src/seed/index.js` 维护注册表；每个业务模块导出 `seedXxx(db)` 幂等函数（按业务键 upsert）。\n- 基础 PR 提供账户种子（alice-dev、bob-reviewer 等，见 docs/seed-data.md）与应用骨架种子。\n- 业务模块在合并时把自己的 seed 注册进总表（`backend/src/seed/all.js` 加一行 import）；\n  **服务器启动时默认执行全部 seed**（`SEED=0` 关闭），因为平台以 `npm run start` 正常启动即需具备初始数据。\n- seed 必须幂等且**不得覆盖用户已产生的数据**：账户种子用 `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`\n  （因此改过密码的账户重启后仍保留新密码）；业务对象按业务键 upsert 同样不得回退用户改动。\n- 场景隔离：验收测试使用唯一后缀对象（`pw-branch-<suffix>` 等，需求原文即如此要求），不做 per-test 重置。\n\n## 7. 前端共享 UI（契约）\n\n- `AppShell`：未登录（logo + Sign in / Sign up）与登录态（全局搜索框、+ 新建下拉、Issues/PR 图标链接、头像账户菜单）。\n- `AccountMenu`：触发按钮 accessible name 为 \"Account menu\"，菜单含当前用户名、组织入口（\"Your organizations\"）、\n  \"Signed in as <user>\"、\"Your repositories\"、\"Settings\"、\"Sign out\"（按需求文本微调以各模块为准）。\n- 表单组件统一输出：label 关联、字段错误（`field` 对应）、aria-describedby 帮助文本。\n- 图标按钮一律 `aria-label`；分段控件计数徽标不进入 accessible name。\n- 路由：`/`、`/login`、`/register`、`/password-reset`、`/settings/*`、`/:owner/:repo(/*)`、`/orgs/:org(/*)`、`/search`。\n  具体页面在业务模块内实现，路由名以需求文本的 URL 语义为准（无需与 GitHub 完全一致）。\n\n## 8. 质量设施（基础 PR 提供）\n\n- Vitest：后端单测（权限、校验规则）；前端组件测试按需。\n- Playwright：`e2e/` 目录，每模块一个 spec 文件；验收用例直接对应需求场景（GIVEN/WHEN/THEN）。\n  断言使用 getByRole + 精确 name。\n- 脚本：`pnpm dev`（portless 并行起前后端）、`pnpm test`、`pnpm e2e`、`pnpm build`。\n\n## 9. 修订记录（advisor 咨询后确定，2026-09-29）\n\n1. **commit 共享不可变**：commit 为全局唯一不可变对象（不按仓库复制）；branch 仅为指针；\n   fork = 在 fork 仓库中创建指向既有 commit 的分支指针；fork 后新 commit 归属 fork 仓库且不影响源仓库。\n   理由：stale review（按 compare commit）、Checks、diff 全部自然落在 commitId 键上；fork 无复制开销。\n2. **合并语义（REQ-6 消费）**：merge base = compare 分支与 base 分支的共同祖先 commit。\n   逐文件三方合并：仅 compare 侧变更 → 取 compare；仅 base 侧变更 → 保持 base；\n   双侧都改且内容不同 → 冲突：该 PR 判定 unmergeable，合并区显示冲突说明且按钮禁用。\n   merge commit 的 tree 在事务内计算并作为新 commit 落库（parents = base head + compare head）。\n3. **前端权限镜像**：后端在仓库/组织作用域的响应中返回 `viewerPermission`（仓库角色或 org 角色枚举）；\n   前端只读取该字段决定控件渲染（隐藏 vs 只读 vs 可操作），不自行计算权限。\n   验收同时断言控件缺席（按需求指明的档次）与服务端拒绝。\n4. **文案契约**：基础 PR 交付 `frontend/src/contracts/copy.ts`（或等价位置），把需求中被断言的\n   accessible name 与错误消息定义为常量；组件与 Playwright 测试都引用它，禁止散落字面量。\n5. **Checks 键**：check 状态按 `(compareCommitId, checkName)` 存储与展示；compare 分支新 commit 后\n   新 compare commit 的必需检查初始为 pending（与 review stale 对称）。\n6. **测试隔离强化**：账户可跨模块共享；业务实体（组织/仓库/issue/PR 等）模块自有 + 唯一 token 命名；\n   列表/搜索/计数断言必须用唯一 token 限定范围，禁止全局计数或排序断言。seed 一律按业务键 upsert、顺序无关。\n7. **依赖库评估记录（better-auth，时间盒结论）**：本产品需要固定验证码找回、字段级多项错误、\n   精确错误文案、仓库级 5 角色授权、团队层级不传播权限、改密/登出即时失效会话。\n   better-auth email/password 与 organization 插件均以自身默认流与角色模型为中心，映射以上需求需大量\n   对抗性定制（覆盖默认错误文案与流程），自建（表结构见 §5）更直接、可控，选定自建。\n8. **better-sqlite3 安装风险**：基础 PR 第一项工作为在平台 npm 环境（PATH=/usr/local/bin:$PATH）做干净\n   `npm install` 实测；失败保留首轮日志并改用有 prebuild 的锁定版本或替代驱动，结论记录在 PR。\n9. **拆分调整**：M4 拆为 M4a（内容模型+浏览+历史+diff）、M4b（分支+代码搜索+Web 编辑）；\n   M6 拆为 M6a（保护规则+Checks+PR 生命周期+三视图）、M6b（行内评论+评审+stale+合并门控）。\n   M6a 硬依赖 M4a 的 diff API；M6b 依赖 M6a。\n10. **基础 PR 提出的 6 项契约增量（根 Issue #1 评论 #8 全部采纳，2026-09-29）**：\n    1. `users.email_verified INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1`：承载 REQ-1-1-1 要求的“已验证邮箱状态”。\n    2. `commit_parents(commit_id, parent_commit_id, position)`：§9.2 的合并 commit 有两个父提交；\n       `commits.parent_id` 保留为第一父。\n    3. `issue_milestones(issue_id PRIMARY KEY, milestone_id)`：一个 issue 至多一个里程碑。\n    4. seed **随启动默认执行**（`SEED=0` 关闭），取代“非生产或显式 SEED=1”；理由与幂等约束见 §6。\n    5. 会话 cookie 不带 `Secure`（HTTP 交付）；理由见 §3。\n    6. 字段错误存在时**始终**返回 `errors[]`（含单条），`error` 为首项；理由见 §3。\n    实现证据：PR #2（`backend/src/db/migrations.js`、`backend/src/http/errors.js`、`backend/test/*`）。\n11. **M1 身份与会话语义澄清（根 Issue #1 评论 #27 采纳 Issue #3 评论 #25 的 D1/D1b/D2，2026-09-29）**：\n    改密保留调用者当前会话、仅吊销该用户其它会话；找回密码吊销该用户全部会话且不自动登录；\n    找回密码不泄露账户存在性（`forgot-password` 对已注册/未注册邮箱响应逐字节一致，`reset-password` 对未知邮箱与\n    错误验证码都返回字段 `code` 的 `Verification code is invalid`，且错误码提交时仍一次返回全部字段错误，\n update`（draft-feature → main） |\n| Check | `test`：pending（Open PR 当前 compare commit） |\n\n## 保护规则 / Checks / 评审\n\n- M6 场景 seed：main 保护规则（可按场景创建，不需要预置时保持空状态页可见）。\n- 评审决定、Checks 更新、reviewers 请求等运行时状态由场景操作产生，不预置。\n\n## 隔离规则\n\n- 跨仓库同名标签/里程碑（bug、Q3 launch 等）必须在\"另一仓库\"也存在一份，用于验证选择器不跨仓库。\n- 验收用 `pw-*` 唯一后缀对象；seed 幂等（业务键 upsert）。\n# 参考截图视觉解读（vision sub-agent 产出摘要）\n\n来源：input/reference/ 27 张 GitHub 截图，由 vision 子代理逐张解读（2026-09-29）。\n需求文本中的精确英文 accessible name 是权威；截图仅作布局/视觉参考。全文见 native run 摘要，此处记录影响分工与验收的结论。\n\n## 布局模式\n- 未登录认证页：居中单列（登录/重置密码），注册页为深色左栏+白色右栏两栏。\n- 登录态全局外壳：顶栏（logo、搜索、图标簇、头像按钮）+ 仓库导航标签（Code/Issues/Pull requests/Settings…）。\n- Issues/PR 列表与里程碑：左侧二级侧边栏 + 主列；仓库概览为左主列+右 About 侧栏。\n- 设置页：左侧设置导航 + 右长表单；Danger Zone 红边框卡片。\n- 风格：白底、1px 浅灰边框小圆角卡片、绿色主按钮、当前标签下划线。\n\n## 可访问性要点（验收相关）\n- 大量纯图标按钮（头像菜单、复制、`+`、齿轮、`...`、关闭 ×、发送等）必须用 aria-label 命名。\n- 分段控件含计数徽标（Open 0 / Closed 0）：计数不得进入控件 accessible name。\n- 同类动作两处名称不同（\"New milestone\" 页眉 vs \"Create a milestone\" 空状态）需分别正确。\n- fork 表单复选框可见文字被内联 code 打断，a11y 名称应为连续文本。\n- 输入框 a11y 名称来自 label，不用 placeholder 顶替。\n\n## 图像缺口（记录为需求语义缺口，按需求文本实现）\n1. github-code-file-browser.png 与 github-repository-overview.png 内容完全相同（仅根目录 README）：\n   目录树/文件详情页无独立视觉参考，布局按 overview + 面包屑语义推断实现。\n2. github-default-branch-settings.png 与 github-branch-protection.png 内容完全相同（保护规则空状态）：\n   默认分支设置区参考 visibility-settings 截图 General 页的 \"Default branch\" 下拉实现。\n3. Web 文件编辑器截图未展示提交信息对话框；提交信息字段与按钮按需求文本（\"Commit message\"、\"Commit changes\"）实现。\n4. 分支保护截图为空状态；规则表单字段按需求文本（Branch name pattern、Require 1 approval、Require status check test、Create/Save changes）实现。\n5. github-account-menu-sign-out.png 实际内容是登录后的 Dashboard 首页：图内没有任何展开的账户菜单或登出对话框，\n   登出确认 UI 无视觉参考（M1 视觉复核，2026-09-29）。\n6. github-password-reset.png / -1.png 两步都没有验证码（code）的任何表现形式（无独立文本、无输入框、无 `123456`），\n   “显示固定验证码 123456”只由需求文本定义；github-password-settings.png 是 “Sign in methods” 列表页（含 Password 行），\n   不含 Current/New/Confirm password 表单，改密表单形态按需求文本实现（M1 视觉复核，2026-09-29）。\n7. 登录/找回/改密码三张图为单列居中、顶部居中标志、整栏宽绿色主按钮、字段为 label 而非 placeholder；\n   注册页是唯一两栏（左侧深色品牌区 + 右侧表单）例外；图中未出现错误提示、成功状态或密码强度指示。\n# 验收方案\n\n## 分层\n\n1. **模块自检（各子 Issue 完成条件）**\n   - 后端：Vitest 覆盖权限矩阵、校验规则（用户名/邮箱/密码/分支名/文件路径/标题长度）、seed 幂等。\n   - 前端 + 集成：Playwright 用例逐条对应需求场景 GIVEN/WHEN/THEN；断言用 `getByRole(role, { name })`\n     精确匹配；覆盖成功路径 + 每条需求列出的失败分支（字段错误文案逐字断言）。\n   - 持久化断言：刷新/重开后状态保持（需求反复出现）。\n2. **整合验收（develop → main 整合 PR，根负责人执行）**\n   - 全量 `pnpm e2e` + 平台启动路径冒烟：frontend `npm install && npm run build`，\n     backend `npm install && HOST=0.0.0.0 PORT=3000 npm run start`，120 秒内可访问。\n   - Node 版本核对：`/usr/local/bin/node --version`（20.19.3）存在且一致时以平台入口为准执行检查。\n\n## 场景隔离与数据\n\n- 每条用例以\"fresh unauthenticated browser session\"开始（新 context）。\n- 写操作用 `pw-<suffix>` 唯一后缀对象；seed 幂等，不做 per-test 重置。\n- 失败时保留 trace、console、请求错误；先区分实现/检查/环境问题，不为迎合实现改判据。\n\n## 精确名称断言抽查（高风险点）\n\n- \"Account menu\"、\"Sign out\"、\"Confirm sign out\"、\"Cancel\"（登出对话框）\n- \"Username already exists\"、\"Verification code is invalid\"、\"Password updated\"\n- \"Create branch: <name>\"、\"Invalid branch\"、\"Branch <name>\"（选择器按钮）\n- \"Add comment\"、\"Add single comment\"、\"Start a review\"、\"Review changes\"、\"Submit review\"\n- \"Merge pull request\"、\"Confirm merge\"、\"Review required by branch protection\"\n- \"Search issues\"、\"Search assignees\"、\"Find branch\"、\"Find a repository\"\n- 选择器 option 的 role=option 与精确 username/label 名称\n# 产品方案（简化版 GitHub 协作平台）\n\n> 根 Issue：local/run#1。本文是所有子任务的共享依据之一；原子需求的权威文本在\n> `/workspace/template/.factory26/20260929-042409-1202e245/input/requirements.yaml`（292KB，含 34 条 ATOMIC 需求与 GIVEN/WHEN/THEN 场景），\n> 参考截图在 `input/reference/`（27 张 GitHub 界面截图）。本文只记录影响分工与验收的决定。\n\n## 1. 产品概述\n\n一个浏览器可用的简化 GitHub 协作平台：账户与会话、组织与团队治理、仓库（搜索/创建/fork/可见性）、\n代码浏览与版本控制（文件、提交、diff、分支、Web 编辑）、Issue 管理、变更评审与合并控制。\n无真实 git 协议、无 Actions/Packages/Wiki/通知/实时协作（需求明确排除）。\n\n## 2. 模块与需求映射\n\n| 模块 | 原子需求 | 关键交付 |\n|---|---|---|\n| M1 身份与访问 | REQ-1-1-1/2/3, REQ-1-2, REQ-1-3 | 注册（字段级错误+保留输入）、登录（统一 \"Invalid credentials\"）、找回密码（固定验证码 123456）、登出（确认对话框）、改密码 |\n| M2 组织与治理 | REQ-2-1-1/2, REQ-2-2-1..4, REQ-2-3 | 组织浏览/创建、团队创建/成员/层级（禁环）、成员增删（last Owner 保护）、按人/团队授予仓库角色 |\n| M3 仓库资产 | REQ-3-1..3-4 | 全局搜索（Enter 直出结果）、创建仓库（含 README 初始化）、fork、Code 克隆值（HTTPS/SSH + Copied）、公开概览、可见性变更 |\n| M4 代码与版本控制（拆分 M4a/M4b） | REQ-4-1, 4-2-1..3, 4-3-1..3, 4-4 | 文件浏览、提交历史（分支/文件范围）、commit diff、代码搜索、分支选择器/创建、默认分支设置、Web 文件编辑产生 commit |\n| M5 Issue 管理 | REQ-5-1-1/2, 5-2-1..3, 5-3-1..3, 5-4 | 列表过滤（Open/Closed 链接、as-you-type）、详情时间线、创建/编辑、评论+表情回应、指派/标签/里程碑侧栏、关闭重开 |\n| M6 评审与合并（拆分 M6a/M6b） | REQ-6-1..6-6 | 分支保护（1 approval + test 检查）、PR 列表/比较/创建/Draft/Ready for review、三视图、行内评论（单条/草稿评审）、评审提交、reviewers、受保护合并、关闭重开 |\n\n## 3. 全局规则（对全部模块生效）\n\n1. **精确可访问名称**：需求中所有引号内的界面名（\"Sign in\"、\"Create account\"、\"Merge pull request\" 等）\n   是控件的精确英文 accessible name；组件库默认文案不得覆盖它们。\n2. **种子数据**：所有提及的账户/组织/团队/仓库/分支/文件/commit/issue/milestone/PR/评审/权限关系为预定义种子，\n   服务端启动前由幂等 seed 写入；对应场景开始前必须存在。种子清单见 `docs/seed-data.md`。\n3. **写入全部服务端持久化 + 事务 + 会话与目标对象权限校验**；失败不得产生部分状态。\n4. **角色不是自动阶梯**：仓库角色 Read<Triage<Write<Maintain<Admin，组织 Owner 视为 Admin；\n   有效角色 = 直接授权与团队授权的最高者；团队层级不传播成员或权限。\n5. **密码规则**：12–128 位，含大写、小写、数字、特殊字符，无空白；用户名规则 1–39 小写字母/数字/单连字符、\n   不以连字符开头结尾（组织名、团队名沿用该风格的变体，见各需求）。\n6. **错误消息文案**：逐条按需求原文（如 \"Username already exists\"、\"Verification code is invalid\"、\n   \"Title is required\"、\"Review required by branch protection\"），在字段旁逐项显示，一次提交可同时显示多项错误。\n7. **权限不足的 UI 行为分三档**：a) 控件完全隐藏（多数写操作）；b) 只读视图；c) 服务端拒绝。\n   每条需求明确指明档次，实现时以需求原文为准。\n\n## 4. 关键用户路径（跨模块主线）\n\n1. 访客 → 注册/登录 → 账户菜单 → 组织/仓库浏览。\n2. 登录用户 → 创建组织 → 建团队/加成员 → 对仓库授权（组织可见性放大）。\n3. 搜索/浏览仓库 → 仓库概览 → Code 文件浏览 → 提交历史/diff → 代码搜索。\n4. 写权限用户 → Web 编辑文件产生 commit → 建分支 → 比较分支 → 建 PR → 评审（行内评论/批准）→\n   Admin 设置保护规则与 Checks → 符合条件后合并。\n5. Triage+ 用户 → Issue 创建/编辑/评论/标签/里程碑/指派 → 关闭重开。\n\n## 5. 重要假设与未决问题\n\n- 演示规模数据（每个仓库数十文件、数百 commit 以内），内容模型采用 commit 级全量文件树快照（决策见 architecture.md）。\n- 邮箱即时视为 verified；无邮件发送。重置验证码固定 123456。\n- \"fresh unauthenticated browser session\" 由验收测试以新浏览器上下文实现；服务端不按会话隔离数据。\n- 未决：见 Issue #1 相关评论（advisor 结论落地后更新）。"

id);\n`,\n  },\n];\n/**\n * Shared permission model (docs/architecture.md §4 — single implementation).\n *\n * Roles are NOT an automatic cumulative ladder: an operation states the role it\n * needs and `roleAtLeast()` compares them in the documented order\n * read < triage < write < maintain < admin. An organisation Owner is an Admin\n * for every repository of that organisation.\n *\n * A user's effective repository role is the highest of\n *   - `admin` when the user owns the repository personally,\n *   - `admin` when the user is an Owner of the owning organisation,\n *   - direct grants on the repository,\n *   - grants to teams the user is a *direct* member of.\n * Team hierarchy never propagates membership or permissions.\n */\n\nimport { forbidden } from '../http/errors.js';\n\nexport const ROLE_ORDER = ['read', 'triage', 'write', 'maintain', 'admin'];\n\nconst ROLE_RANK = new Map(ROLE_ORDER.map((role, index) => [role, index]));\n\n/** @param {string|null|undefined} role */\nexport function isRepoRole(role) {\n  return typeof role === 'string' && ROLE_RANK.has(role);\n}\n\n/** Highest of two roles (null-safe). */\nexport function maxRole(a, b) {\n  const rankA = isRepoRole(a) ? ROLE_RANK.get(a) : -1;\n  const rankB = isRepoRole(b) ? ROLE_RANK.get(b) : -1;\n  if (rankA < 0 && rankB < 0) return null;\n  return rankA >= rankB ? a : b;\n}\n\n/** Does `role` satisfy `required`? Null role never satisfies anything. */\nexport function roleAtLeast(role, required) {\n  if (!isRepoRole(role) || !isRepoRole(required)) return false;\n  return ROLE_RANK.get(role) >= ROLE_RANK.get(required);\n}\n\n/**\n * Effective role of a user on a repository.\n *\n * @param {import('better-sqlite3').Database} db\n * @param {{id:number}|null} user  public or raw user row (null = anonymous)\n * @param {{id:number, owner_user_id:number|null, owner_org_id:number|null}} repo\n * @returns {'read'|'triage'|'write'|'maintain'|'admin'|null}\n */\nexport function effectiveRepoRole(db, user, repo) {\n  if (!user || !repo) return null;\n  if (repo.owner_user_id != null && Number(repo.owner_user_id) === Number(user.id)) return 'admin';\n\n  let role = null;\n\n  if (repo.owner_org_id != null) {\n    const membership = db\n      .prepare('SELECT role FROM org_members WHERE org_id = ? AND user_id = ?')\n      .get(repo.owner_org_id, user.id);\n    if (membership?.role === 'owner') return 'admin';\n  }\n\n  const direct = db\n    .prepare('SELECT role FROM repo_grants WHERE repo_id = ? AND subject_user_id = ?')\n    .get(repo.id, user.id);\n  if (direct) role = maxRole(role, direct.role);\n\n  // Direct team memberships only — hierarchy does not propagate permissions.\n  const teamGrants = db\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT g.role AS role\n         FROM repo_grants g\n         JOIN team_members tm ON tm.team_id = g.subject_team_id\n        WHERE g.repo_id = ? AND tm.user_id = ?`,\n    )\n    .all(repo.id, user.id);\n  for (const grant of teamGrants) role = maxRole(role, grant.role);\n\n  return role;\n}\n\n/**\n * Role of a user inside an organisation ('owner' | 'member' | null).\n */\nexport function orgRole(db, user, orgId) {\n  if (!user || orgId == null) return null;\n  const membership = db\n    .prepare('SELECT role FROM org_members WHERE org_id = ? AND user_id = ?')\n    .get(orgId, user.id);\n  return membership?.role ?? null;\n}\n\n/** Can the user see the repository at all (public repositories are readable)? */\nexport function canViewRepo(db, user, repo) {\n  if (!repo) return false;\n  if (repo.visibility === 'public') return true;\n  return effectiveRepoRole(db, user, repo) != null;\n}\n\n/**\n * The `viewerPermission` value attached to repository/organisation responses\n * (docs/architecture.md §9.3). The frontend only reads this field; it never\n * computes permissions itself.\n *\n * @returns {{ repository?: string|null, organization?: string|null, canView: boolean }}\n */\nexport function viewerPermissionForRepo(db, user, repo) {\n  return {\n    repository: repo ? effectiveRepoRole(db, user, repo) : null,\n    organization: repo?.owner_org_id != null ? orgRole(db, user, repo.owner_org_id) : null,\n    canView: canViewRepo(db, user, repo),\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Express middleware factory for repository-scoped routes. The route must have\n * loaded the repository onto `req.repo`.\n * @param {'read'|'triage'|'write'|'maintain'|'admin'} required\n */\nexport function requireRepoRole(db, required) {\n  return (req, res, next) => {\n    const repo = req.repo;\n    if (!repo) {\n      next(new Error('requireRepoRole() needs req.repo to be loaded first'));\n      return;\n    }\n    const role = effectiveRepoRole(db, req.user, repo);\n    if (!roleAtLeast(role, required)) {\n      next(forbidden());\n      return;\n    }\n    req.repoRole = role;\n    next();\n  };\n}\n/**\n * Import every seed module exactly once, then run the registry.\n *\n * Business modules append one import line here (or import this file from their\n * own bootstrap) so their `registerSeed(...)` calls are executed.\n */\nimport './accounts.js';\n\nexport { registerSeed, runSeeds, registeredSeeds, resetSeedRegistry } from './index.js';\n/**\n * Seed registry (docs/architecture.md §6).\n *\n * Each business module exports an idempotent `seedXxx(db)` function and\n * registers it here; `runSeeds()` executes the registry inside transactions at\n * server startup. Seeds are keyed by business identity and must be safe to run\n * repeatedly without overwriting data the user already produced (for example an\n * account whose password was changed through the UI must keep its new password\n * after a restart).\n */\nimport { transact } from '../db/index.js';\n\n/** @type {Map<string, (db: import('better-sqlite3').Database) => void>} */\nconst registry = new Map();\n\n/**\n * @param {string} name unique seed name, e.g. 'accounts'\n * @param {(db: import('better-sqlite3').Database) => void} fn idempotent seed body\n */\nexport function registerSeed(name, fn) {\n  if (registry.has(name)) {\n    throw new Error(`Seed \"${name}\" is already registered`);\n  }\n  registry.set(name, fn);\n}\n\nexport function registeredSeeds() {\n  return [...registry.keys()];\n}\n\n/**\n * Run every registered seed. A failure rolls back only the failing seed, so a\n * broken module seed cannot corrupt data written by the others.\n * @returns {string[]} names that ran\n */\nexport function runSeeds(db, { logger = console } = {}) {\n  const ran = [];\n  for (const [name, fn] of registry) {\n    try {\n      transact(db, () => fn(db));\n      ran.push(name);\n    } catch (error) {\n      logger.error(`[seed] \"${name}\" failed:`, error);\n      throw error;\n    }\n  }\n  return ran;\n}\n\n/** Test helper: clear the registry (only used between isolated test suites). */\nexport function resetSeedRegistry() {\n  registry.clear();\n}\n/**\n * Foundation seed: the accounts listed in docs/seed-data.md.\n *\n * Idempotent by username: if the account already exists nothing is written, so a\n * password changed through the UI survives a restart of the application.\n */\nimport { hashPassword } from '../auth/password.js';\nimport { registerSeed } from './index.js';\n\n/** Shared password for every seeded account (docs/seed-data.md). */\nexport const SEED_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD = 'Valid-password-123!';\n\nexport const SEED_ACCOUNTS = [\n  {\n    username: 'alice-dev',\n    email: 'alice.dev@example.test',\n    note: 'Primary account; owner of the Acme Demo organisation and of acme-docs/secret-research',\n  },\n  {\n    username: 'bob-reviewer',\n    email: 'bob.review@example.test',\n    note: 'Acme Demo member; Write collaborator on acme-docs',\n  },\n  {\n    username: 'carol-writer',\n    email: 'carol.writer@example.test',\n    note: 'Registered user who is not an organisation member',\n  },\n  {\n    username: 'dave-reader',\n    email: 'dave.reader@example.test',\n    note: 'Plain read-only collaborator (permission counter-example)',\n  },\n  {\n    username: 'erin-admin',\n    email: 'erin.admin@example.test',\n    note: 'Repository Admin account',\n  },\n];\n\n/** @param {import('better-sqlite3').Database} db */\nexport function seedAccounts(db) {\n  const passwordHash = hashPassword(SEED_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD);\n  const insert = db.prepare(\n    `INSERT INTO users (username, email, password_hash, email_verified)\n     VALUES (@username, @email, @password_hash, 1)\n     ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`,\n  );\n  for (const account of SEED_ACCOUNTS) {\n    insert.run({\n      username: account.username,\n      email: account.email,\n      password_hash: passwordHash,\n    });\n  }\n}\n\nregisterSeed('accounts', seedAccounts);"

perform this action') {\n  return new ApiError(403, 'forbidden', message);\n}\n\n/** 404 helper. */\nexport function notFound(message = 'Not found') {\n  return new ApiError(404, 'not_found', message);\n}\n\n/**\n * Express error middleware. Must be registered last.\n * Unknown errors become a generic 500 without leaking internals.\n */\nexport function errorHandler() {\n  // eslint-disable-next-line no-unused-vars -- Express needs the 4-argument shape\n  return (err, req, res, next) => {\n    if (err instanceof ApiError) {\n      res.status(err.status).json(err.toBody());\n      return;\n    }\n    if (err && err.type === 'entity.parse.failed') {\n      res.status(400).json({\n        error: { code: 'invalid_json', message: 'Request body must be valid JSON' },\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n    req.log?.error?.(err);\n    console.error('[api] unhandled error:', err);\n    res.status(500).json({\n      error: { code: 'internal_error', message: 'Something went wrong' },\n    });\n  };\n}\n\n/** JSON 404 for unknown /api routes. */\nexport function apiNotFoundHandler() {\n  return (req, res) => {\n    res.status(404).json({ error: { code: 'not_found', message: 'Not found' } });\n  };\n}\n/**\n * Shared input validation (single implementation, docs/architecture.md §5).\n *\n * Every validator returns `{ ok: true, value }` or `{ ok: false, code, message, field }`.\n * Routes collect the failures of all submitted fields and report them together\n * (`validationError(errors)`), so one submission can show several field errors.\n */\nimport { messages } from '../contracts/messages.js';\n\nconst USERNAME_RE = /^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;\nconst EMAIL_LOCAL_RE = /^[^\\s@]+$/;\n\n/** @param {unknown} raw */\nfunction text(raw) {\n  return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw : raw === undefined || raw === null ? '' : String(raw);\n}\n\n/** Username: 1–39 lowercase letters/digits/single hyphens, no leading/trailing hyphen. */\nexport function validateUsername(raw, field = 'username') {\n  const value = text(raw).trim();\n  if (!value) {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'username_required', message: messages.USERNAME_REQUIRED, field };\n  }\n  if (value.length > 39 || !USERNAME_RE.test(value)) {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'username_format_invalid', message: messages.USERNAME_FORMAT_INVALID, field };\n  }\n  return { ok: true, value, field };\n}\n\n/**\n * Email: trimmed, exactly one `@`, at most 254 characters, and at least one dot\n * with non-empty domain labels after the `@`.\n */\nexport function validateEmail(raw, field = 'email') {\n  const value = text(raw).trim().toLowerCase();\n  if (!value) {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'email_required', message: messages.EMAIL_REQUIRED, field };\n  }\n  const parts = value.split('@');\n  const invalid = () => ({ ok: false, code: 'email_format_invalid', message: messages.EMAIL_FORMAT_INVALID, field });\n  if (value.length > 254 || parts.length !== 2) return invalid();\n  const [local, domain] = parts;\n  if (!EMAIL_LOCAL_RE.test(local)) return invalid();\n  const labels = domain.split('.');\n  if (labels.length < 2 || labels.some((label) => label.length === 0)) return invalid();\n  if (/\\s/.test(domain)) return invalid();\n  return { ok: true, value, field };\n}\n\n/**\n * Password: 12–128 characters with at least one uppercase letter, one lowercase\n * letter, one digit and one non-alphanumeric character, and no whitespace.\n */\nexport function validatePassword(raw, { field = 'password', requiredMessage = messages.PASSWORD_REQUIRED } = {}) {\n  const value = text(raw);\n  if (!value) {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'password_required', message: requiredMessage, field };\n  }\n  const invalid = () => ({\n    ok: false,\n    code: 'password_requirements_not_satisfied',\n    message: messages.PASSWORD_REQUIREMENTS,\n    field,\n  });\n  if (value.length < 12 || value.length > 128) return invalid();\n  if (/\\s/.test(value)) return invalid();\n  if (!/[A-Z]/.test(value)) return invalid();\n  if (!/[a-z]/.test(value)) return invalid();\n  if (!/[0-9]/.test(value)) return invalid();\n  if (!/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(value)) return invalid();\n  return { ok: true, value, field };\n}\n\n/** Confirmation field must equal the original character for character. */\nexport function validateConfirmation(raw, original, field = 'confirmPassword') {\n  const value = text(raw);\n  if (!value) {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'password_confirmation_required', message: messages.PASSWORD_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED, field };\n  }\n  if (value !== text(original)) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      code: 'password_confirmation_mismatch',\n      message: messages.PASSWORD_CONFIRMATION_MISMATCH,\n      field,\n    };\n  }\n  return { ok: true, value, field };\n}\n\n/** Terms checkbox must be accepted (registration). */\nexport function validateTerms(raw, field = 'agreeToTerms') {\n  const accepted = raw === true || raw === 'true' || raw === 'on' || raw === 1 || raw === '1';\n  if (!accepted) {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'terms_required', message: messages.TERMS_REQUIRED, field };\n  }\n  return { ok: true, value: true, field };\n}\n\n/** Organisation / team name: same character rules as usernames. */\nexport function validateOrgName(raw, field = 'name') {\n  return validateUsername(raw, field);\n}\n\n/**\n * Branch names (REQ-4). Rejects the git-invalid forms the requirements call out:\n * empty, leading/trailing dot or slash, `..`, `~ ^ : ? * [ \\`, `@{`, trailing `.lock`.\n */\nexport function validateBranchName(raw, field = 'branch') {\n  const value = text(raw).trim();\n  const invalid = () => ({ ok: false, code: 'invalid_branch', message: messages.INVALID_BRANCH, field });\n  if (!value || value.length > 255) return invalid();\n  if (value === '@') return invalid();\n  if (value.startsWith('/') || value.endsWith('/') || value.endsWith('.')) return invalid();\n  if (value.includes('..') || value.includes('//') || value.includes('@{')) return invalid();\n  if (/[\\s~^:?*\\[\\\\]/.test(value)) return invalid();\n  if (value.endsWith('.lock')) return invalid();\n  if (value.startsWith('.') || value.split('/').some((part) => part.startsWith('.'))) return invalid();\n  return { ok: true, value, field };\n}\n\n/**\n * Repository file paths (Web editor, REQ-4-3): never absolute, no `..` segment,\n * no empty segment, no control characters.\n */\nexport function validateFilePath(raw, field = 'path') {\n  const value = text(raw).trim();\n  const invalid = () => ({ ok: false, code: 'invalid_file_path', message: messages.INVALID_FILE_PATH, field });\n  if (!value || value.length > 4096) return invalid();\n  if (value.startsWith('/') || value.endsWith('/')) return invalid();\n  const segments = value.split('/');\n  if (segments.some((segment) => segment === '' || segment === '.' || segment === '..')) return invalid();\n  if (/[\\u0000-\\u001f\\\\]/.test(value)) return invalid();\n  return { ok: true, value, field };\n}\n\n/** Run several validators and collect only the failures, in submission order. */\nexport function collectErrors(results) {\n  return results.filter((result) => !result.ok).map(({ code, message, field }) => ({ code, message, field }));\n}\n/**\n * Authentication middleware.\n *\n * `attachUser` resolves the session cookie (if any) and exposes `req.user`\n * (public user shape) + `req.sessionId` to every route, including public ones,\n * so responses can carry `viewerPermission`.\n * `requireAuth` rejects anonymous requests with the uniform 401 body.\n */\nimport { getSessionUser, readSessionId, publicUser } from './session.js';\nimport { unauthorized } from '../http/errors.js';\n\nexport function attachUser(db) {\n  return (req, res, next) => {\n    const sessionId = readSessionId(req);\n    const user = sessionId ? getSessionUser(db, sessionId) : null;\n    req.sessionId = user ? sessionId : null;\n    req.user = user ? publicUser(user) : null;\n    // The internal row is handy for permission code that needs the raw id.\n    req.userRow = user || null;\n    next();\n  };\n}\n\nexport function requireAuth() {\n  return (req, res, next) => {\n    if (!req.user) {\n      next(unauthorized());\n      return;\n    }\n    next();\n  };\n}"

(\n        <p className=\"mt-3\">\n          <Link className=\"text-accent\" to=\"/login\">\n            {authCopy.signIn}\n          </Link>\n        </p>\n      ) : null}\n      <p className=\"mt-3\">\n        <Link className=\"text-accent\" to=\"/\">\n          Home\n        </Link>\n      </p>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default RepositoryMissing;\nimport { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { useAuth } from '../../auth/AuthContext';\nimport { repoApi } from './api';\nimport type { RepositoryDetail } from './api';\n\nexport type RepositoryState =\n  | { status: 'loading' }\n  | { status: 'ready'; repository: RepositoryDetail }\n  | { status: 'missing' };\n\n/**\n * Loads one repository overview payload.\n *\n * A repository the viewer may not see answers 404 (never a different error), so\n * \"not found\" and \"no access\" share one state and no private repository is\n * disclosed. The request is repeated when the session changes, because visibility\n * and `viewerPermission` depend on the viewer.\n */\nexport function useRepository(owner: string, name: string): RepositoryState {\n  const { user, loading } = useAuth();\n  const [state, setState] = useState<RepositoryState>({ status: 'loading' });\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (loading) return;\n    let active = true;\n    setState({ status: 'loading' });\n    repoApi\n      .detail(owner, name)\n      .then((repository) => {\n        if (active) setState({ status: 'ready', repository });\n      })\n      .catch(() => {\n        if (active) setState({ status: 'missing' });\n      });\n    return () => {\n      active = false;\n    };\n  }, [owner, name, loading, user?.id]);\n\n  return state;\n}\nimport { useState } from 'react';\nimport { Link, useLocation, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { Button } from '../../components/Button';\nimport { Dialog } from '../../components/Dialog';\nimport { ErrorAlertList } from '../../components/Alerts';\nimport { RadioGroup } from '../../components/RadioGroup';\nimport { TextField } from '../../components/TextField';\nimport { repo as repoCopy, errors as errorCopy } from '../../contracts/copy';\nimport { cx } from '../../utils/cx';\nimport { formErrors, repoApi } from './api';\nimport type { Visibility } from './api';\nimport { RepoHeader, RepoNav } from './RepoHeader';\nimport { RepositoryMissing } from './RepositoryMissing';\nimport { useRepository } from './useRepository';\n\n/**\n * Repository settings (REQ-3-4).\n *\n * \"Settings\" → \"General\" shows the repository summary and, for an Admin only, the\n * Danger Zone with the \"Change visibility\" button. The confirmation dialog offers\n * the \"Public\"/\"Private\" radios and \"Confirm visibility\"; the repository name is\n * never *required* to be retyped (Issue #5 comment #20), but an optional\n * confirmation input lets a user type it — and rejects a mismatch, which is the\n * other branch REQ-3-4 describes. A non-Admin collaborator reaches this page but\n * never sees the visibility action; the server rejects the change as well.\n */\nexport function RepoSettingsPage() {\n  const params = useParams();\n  const owner = params.owner ?? '';\n  const name = params.repo ?? '';\n  const state = useRepository(owner, name);\n  const location = useLocation();\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const [dialogOpen, setDialogOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [nextVisibility, setNextVisibility] = useState<Visibility>('public');\n  const [confirmation, setConfirmation] = useState('');\n  const [confirmationError, setConfirmationError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [general, setGeneral] = useState<string[]>([]);\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n\n  if (state.status === 'loading') {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"page-shell\">\n        <p className=\"text-muted\">Loading…</p>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n  if (state.status === 'missing') return <RepositoryMissing owner={owner} name={name} />;\n\n  const current = state.repository;\n  const isAdmin = current.viewerPermission.repository === 'admin';\n  const settingsBase = `/${current.owner}/${current.name}/settings`;\n  const onGeneral = isGeneral(location.pathname, settingsBase);\n\n  const openDialog = () => {\n    setNextVisibility(current.visibility === 'private' ? 'public' : 'private');\n    setConfirmation('');\n    setConfirmationError(null);\n    setGeneral([]);\n    setDialogOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const confirm = async () => {\n    const typed = confirmation.trim();\n    if (typed && typed !== current.name && typed !== `${current.owner}/${current.name}`) {\n      setConfirmationError(errorCopy.visibilityConfirmationMismatch);\n      return;\n    }\n    setConfirmationError(null);\n    setBusy(true);\n    setGeneral([]);\n    try {\n      await repoApi.changeVisibility(current.owner, current.name, nextVisibility);\n      setDialogOpen(false);\n      // The requirement asserts the marker on the repository overview page.\n      navigate(`/${current.owner}/${current.name}`);\n    } catch (error) {\n      setGeneral(formErrors(error).general);\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);"
      }

[query] : [];\\n}\\n',\n    'docs/guide.md': '# Guide\\n\\nHow to browse the acme-docs repository.\\n',\n  },\n};\n\nfunction userByUsername(db, username) {\n  const row = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE username = ?').get(username);\n  if (!row) throw new Error(`seed(repos): account \"${username}\" must exist before the repository seed runs`);\n  return row;\n}\n\nfunction ensureOrganisation(db, name, displayName) {\n  db.prepare('INSERT INTO organizations (name, display_name) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING').run(\n    name,\n    displayName,\n  );\n  return db.prepare('SELECT * FROM organizations WHERE name = ?').get(name);\n}\n\nfunction ensureOrgMember(db, orgId, userId, role) {\n  db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO org_members (org_id, user_id, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').run(orgId, userId, role);\n}\n\n/** Insert-if-absent by business key; returns the (possibly pre-existing) row. */\nfunction ensureRepository(db, { ownerUserId = null, ownerOrgId = null, name, description = '', visibility, forkSourceId = null }) {\n  const existing =\n    ownerUserId != null\n      ? db.prepare('SELECT * FROM repos WHERE owner_user_id = ? AND name = ?').get(ownerUserId, name)\n      : db.prepare('SELECT * FROM repos WHERE owner_org_id = ? AND name = ?').get(ownerOrgId, name);\n  if (existing) return existing;\n  return db\n    .prepare(\n      `INSERT INTO repos (owner_user_id, owner_org_id, name, description, visibility, fork_source_id)\n       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`,\n    )\n    .get(ownerUserId, ownerOrgId, name, description, visibility, forkSourceId);\n}\n\n/** Commits are keyed by (repository, message) so re-seeding is idempotent. */\nfunction ensureCommit(db, { repoId, authorId, message, tree, parentId = null, files = [] }) {\n  const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM commits WHERE repo_id = ? AND message = ?').get(repoId, message);\n  if (existing) return existing;\n  const commit = db\n    .prepare(\n      `INSERT INTO commits (repo_id, parent_id, author_id, message, tree_json)\n       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`,\n    )\n    .get(repoId, parentId, authorId, message, JSON.stringify(tree));\n  const insertFile = db.prepare(\n    'INSERT INTO commit_files (commit_id, path, status, additions, deletions) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',\n  );\n  for (const file of files) insertFile.run(commit.id, file.path, file.status, file.additions ?? 0, file.deletions ?? 0);\n  return commit;\n}\n\nfunction ensureBranch(db, { repoId, name, headCommitId }) {\n  const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM branches WHERE repo_id = ? AND name = ?').get(repoId, name);\n  if (existing) return existing;\n  return db\n    .prepare('INSERT INTO branches (repo_id, name, head_commit_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?) RETURNING *')\n    .get(repoId, name, headCommitId);\n}\n\n/** Only fills a missing pointer: a default branch a user changed stays changed. */\nfunction ensureDefaultBranch(db, repo, branch) {\n  if (repo.default_branch_id != null) return;\n  db.prepare('UPDATE repos SET default_branch_id = ? WHERE id = ? AND default_branch_id IS NULL').run(\n    branch.id,\n    repo.id,\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ensureGrant(db, repoId, userId, role) {\n  db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO repo_grants (repo_id, subject_user_id, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').run(\n    repoId,\n    userId,\n    role,\n  );\n}\n\n/** Repository + default branch + one initial commit with the given files. */\nfunction seedSimpleRepository(db, { ownerUserId, ownerOrgId, name, description, visibility, authorId, message, tree }) {\n  const repo = ensureRepository(db, { ownerUserId, ownerOrgId, name, description, visibility });\n  const commit = ensureCommit(db, {\n    repoId: repo.id,\n    authorId,\n    message,\n    tree,\n    files: Object.keys(tree).map((path) => ({ path, status: 'added', additions: 1, deletions: 0 })),\n  });\n  const branch = ensureBranch(db, { repoId: repo.id, name: 'main', headCommitId: commit.id });\n  ensureDefaultBranch(db, repo, branch);\n  return repo;\n}\n\nfunction additions: 2, deletions: 0 },\n    ],\n  });\n\n  const searchFlow = ensureCommit(db, {\n    repoId: repo.id,\n    authorId: alice.id,\n    message: 'Document search flow',\n    tree: ACME_DOCS_TREES.searching,\n    parentId: initial.id,\n    files: [\n      { path: 'src/search.ts', status: 'modified', additions: 1, deletions: 1 },\n      { path: 'README.md', status: 'modified', additions: 1, deletions: 1 },\n    ],\n  });\n\n  const main = ensureBranch(db, { repoId: repo.id, name: 'main', headCommitId: searchFlow.id });\n  ensureDefaultBranch(db, repo, main);\n\n  // 'feature-search', headCommitId: featureCommit.id });\n\n  return repo;\n}\n\n/** @param {import('better-sqlite3').Database} db */\nexport function seedRepositories(db) {\n  const alice = Keyboard support covers Enter/Space on the trigger, Escape to\n * close (focus returns to the trigger) and Tab/Shift+Tab through the entries.\n */\nexport function Menu({ trigger, children, align = 'end' }: MenuProps) {\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\n  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);\n  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n\n  const close = useCallback(() => {\n    setOpen(false);\n    triggerRef.current?.focus();\n  }, []);\n\n  const toggle = useCallback(() => setOpen((value) => !value), []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!open) return;\n    const onPointerDown = (event: MouseEvent) => {\n      const target = event.target as Node;\n      if (panelRef.current?.contains(target) || triggerRef.current?.contains(target)) return;\n      setOpen(false);\n    };\n    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (event.key === 'Escape') {\n        event.stopPropagation();\n        close();\n      }\n    };\n    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onPointerDown);\n    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);\n    return () => {\n      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onPointerDown);\n      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, [open, close]);\n\n  const api: MenuRenderApi = { open, toggle, close, triggerRef };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"relative\">\n      {trigger(api)}\n      {open ? true : undefined}\n        {...rest}\n      />\n    </FieldFrame>\n  );\n}\n\nexport interface TextAreaFieldProps extends TextareaHTMLAttributes<HTMLTextAreaElement> {\n  label: string;\n  error?: string | null;\n  help?: string;\n}\n\n/** Labelled textarea — shared by Issue/PR description forms. */\nexport function TextAreaField({ label, error, help, id, className, ...rest }: TextAreaFieldProps) {\n  const generatedId = useId();\n  const fieldId = id ?? --server-cwd backend\n *   node e2e/run.mjs --grep \"sign out\"  # forward a Playwright grep\n */\nimport { spawn } from 'node:child_process';\nimport fs from 'node:fs';\nimport net from 'node:net';\nimport os from 'node:os';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';\nimport { nodeVersion, resolveAppNode } from '../scripts/app-node.mjs';\n\nconst rootDir = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');\nconst appNode = resolveAppNode();\n\nfunction parseArgs(argv) {\n  const options = { build: true, server: `${appNode} src/server.js`, serverCwd: 'backend', grep: null };\n  for (let index = 0; index < argv.length; index += 1) {\n    const arg = argv[index];\n    if (arg === '--no-build' || arg === '--reuse-build') options.build = false;\n    else if (arg === '--server') options.server = argv[++index];\n    else if (arg === '--server-cwd') options.serverCwd = argv[++index];\n    else if (arg === '--grep') options.grep = argv[++index];\n    else if (arg === '--help') {\n      console.log('see the header of e2e/run.mjs for the supported options');\n      process.exit(0);\n    }\n  }\n  return options;\n}\n\nfunction freePort() {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const server = net.createServer();\n    server.unref();\n    server.on('error', reject);\n    server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', () => {\n      const { port } = server.address();\n      server.close(() => resolve(port));\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction run(command, { cwd, env, label }) {\n  return new Promise((resolve) => {\n    const child = spawn(command, { cwd, env, shell: true, stdio: 'inherit' });\n    child.on('close', (code, signal) => resolve({ code: code ?? (signal ? 1 : 0), signal }));\n    child.on('error', (error) => {\n      console.error(`[e2e] failed to start ${label}:`, error.message);\n      resolve({ code: 1, signal: null });\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nasync function waitForHealth(baseUrl, child, timeoutMs = 60_000) {\n  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;\n  while (Date.now() < deadline) {\n    if (child.exitCode !== null) return false;\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/health`);\n      if (response.ok) return true;\n    } catch {\n      /* not up yet */\n    }\n    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 300));\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\nimport { initDb } from '../src/db/index.js';\nimport '../src/seed/all.js';\nimport { runSeeds } from '../src/seed/index.js';\n\n/** Fresh in-memory database with migrations + seed data applied. */\nexport function createTestDb({ seed = true } = {}) {\n  const db = initDb(':memory:');\n  if (seed) runSeeds(db, { logger: { error: () => {} } });\n  return db;\n}\n\n/** Insert a user directly (test convenience). */\nexport function createUser(db, { username, email, passwordHash = 'x', verified = 1 }) {\n  return db\n    .prepare(\n      `INSERT INTO users (username, email, password_hash, email_verified)\n       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`,\n    )\n    .get(username, email ?? `${username}@example.test`, passwordHash, verified);\n}\n\n/** Start the real HTTP server on an ephemeral port; returns base url + teardown. */\nexport async function startTestServer({ dbPath = ':memory:', seed = true } = {}) {\n  const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.js');\n  const { server, db } = startServer({\n    overrides: { dbPath, port: 0, host: '127.0.0.1', runSeeds: seed },\n  });\n  await new Promise((resolve) => server.once('listening', resolve));\n  const { port } = server.address();\n  return {\n    baseUrl: `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`,\n    db,\n    async close() {\n      await new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve));\n      db.close();\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n/** Minimal cookie-aware JSON client for integration tests. */\nexport function createClient(baseUrl) {\n  const cookies = new Map();\n  const cookieHeader = () => [...cookies.entries()].map(([k, v]) => `${k}=${v}`).join('; ');\n\n  async function request(method, path, body) {\n    const headers = {};\n    if (body !== undefined) headers['content-type'] = 'application/json';\n    if (cookies.size) headers.cookie = cookieHeader();\n    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}${path}`, {\n      method,\n      headers,\n      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),\n    });\n    for (const raw of response.headers.getSetCookie?.() ?? []) {\n      const [pair] = raw.split(';');\n      const index = pair.indexOf('=');\n      const name = pair.slice(0, index);\n      const value = pair.slice(index + 1);\n      if (value === '') cookies.delete(name);\n      else cookies.set(name, value);\n    }\n    const textBody = await response.text();\n    let json = null;\n    try {\n      json = textBody ? JSON.parse(textBody) : null;\n    } catch {\n      json = null;\n    }\n    return { status: response.status, body: json, text: textBody, headers: response.headers };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    get: (path) => request('GET', path),\n    post: (path, body) => request('POST', path, body),\n    hasSessionCookie: () => cookies.size > 0,\n  };\n}"
